Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **First request:** design the empire value Φ for a future campaign (adventure-map) AI for Heroes 3 on VCMI.
     - Objective: maximize total_value_of_my_empire_after_N_days / total_value_of_all_empires_after_N_days, with the lowest possible N that still gives a game-winning policy. N will probably be ≤ 7 days, maybe only 2.
     - Φ should "include a LOT of things" so that a small N doesn't create dumb failure modes (e.g. a bias for unneeded actions).
     - Φ should make decisions "roughly *balanced*" when possible, with no strong starting bias. The user's examples:
       - a Capitol costing 10k is valued at 10k;
       - a dwelling plus its available unbought creatures equals the dwelling's cost, so it turns positive only when growth arrives;
       - not every choice can be neutral (the treasure chest offers 1000/500, 1500/1000 or 2000/1500 gold/XP);
       - maybe the main hero's levels are worth a lot and other heroes' levels less.
     - I answered in chat (summarized in §5).
   - **Second request (current):** "after you are done, you will also list all decision types you can make on the campaign" at type granularity. The user's examples: build in town, trade in market, move hero, choose gold/xp for treasure, choose to engage retreating enemies, choose to fight a map feature (like Dragon Utopia) after seeing the defenders' description. Then "devise the best campaign map training procedure put everything you think of here in a doc for me to review. I expect there will be many things to improve, prove me wrong."
     - I read "after you are done" as accepting my earlier offer to write the value design into docs/.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Potential-based shaping** (Ng/Harada/Russell 1999).
     - Daily reward R_p(d+1) − R_p(d), where R = Φ_p/ΣΦ. The rewards telescope to R_end − R_start, and R_end is 1 or 0 at elimination.
     - The N-day objective is exactly truncation with a zero critic. A critic learning the residual extends the horizon.
   - **Prices:** list prices in gold; resources at config/resources.json prices (wood/ore 250, the rest 500). A purchase at list price leaves Φ unchanged; market trades lose value through the spread.
   - **Army premium α (uniform across creatures):**
     - hired creatures = (1+α)·cost × command factor;
     - creatures available to buy = α·min(available + accrued, budget), where budget = gold + income until the next growth day (complementarity);
     - a dwelling's value = cost − α·first batch;
     - growth accrues daily by calendar, (weekday−1)/7 of next Monday's batch.
   - **Unpriced income** (village hall, mines, Estates, grail gold) = 5× daily income. Town Hall, City Hall and Capitol each cost exactly 5 days of the income they add.
   - **Hero command factor:** k_s = 1 + (1−β)(m_own−1) + β(m*−1).
     - m comes from the damage formula with Lanchester's square root: +1 attack or defense ≈ 1.3–2.5% effective army; a level ≈ 3–5%.
     - The best hero also gets a forward-army term W.
     - Cross-check: the RMG prices are 1500 per primary point, 2500 per level, 1.5 gold/XP.
   - **Threat discount:** p = σ(k·ln(S_enemy/S_mine)) for assets an enemy can reach next turn.
     - It is measured right after the threatened player's own turn.
     - Each enemy hero counts against one target only.
     - Towns and artifacts transfer to the attacker; armies are destroyed.
   - **Free values** (α, β, W, k, fort factors, mana price, income multiple) are fitted so the share has no predictable drift. A residual regression on each component's share then shows any mispriced component.
   - **Checks before use:** no-arbitrage tests, balance tests, and a weekday check.
   - **Multiplayer:** dR ∝ (Σ−m)Δm − m·Δo.
   - **Training plan:**
     - our own thin C++ AI (AlphaMapAI) as executor, with pointer/entity-token actions;
     - NK2 as teacher (logged calls plus goal object, with DART noise) and as opponent;
     - a privileged separate critic: sigmoid + BCE on the final share, with logit offset logit(R);
     - GAE with λ applied per day, γ=1;
     - PPO at LR 1e-4 with KL control, a KL penalty towards the imitation policy, per-seat advantage normalization, and entropy with detached parent probabilities;
     - an exploration floor, plus 7-day episodes started from saves (--testsave);
     - a league with NK2 kept as an anchor, and paired evaluation.

- **Latest message (the one being acted on):** detailed feedback that overrides the proposal, then "start working… build it now." It contains:
  - **Reward and labels:**
    - No same-day rewards. The label for the end of day 3 comes from day 5, or a trio such as days 5, 9 and 11.
    - Post-elimination labels equal the label of the elimination day.
    - Play until the game ends, maybe with a day cap.
    - The campaign is multiplayer from the start.
  - **Creatures:**
    - No command factor; buying creatures should be neutral.
    - No accrued growth.
    - The AI should learn calendar timing (build dwellings Saturday, City Hall Sunday): "do not smooth it out."
    - A short horizon biasing against army is "a good thing for some N".
  - **Heroes:** use the combat AI's hero valuation. Hiring should not always be neutral. Maybe a small quadratic penalty on hero count. Monitor heroes with a single sane number.
  - **Shared value list:** artifacts and heroes come from one list shared by the combat and campaign AIs. The campaign may change it most later.
  - **Threat:** not valued; it should be learned. That is why the label starts at d+2.
  - **Mana:** a tiny value: spell power × the mana a hero can spend in a 3-round combat.
  - **Exploration:** priced at maybe 10 gc per tile. Concern: don't let Cover of Darkness reward camping next to a necropolis.
  - **Income multiple:** 5 is sane.
  - **Move:** only tiles within movement range AND within K tiles (a fixed search space is OK).
  - **Removed or reassigned decisions:**
    - "put a hero to sleep" is not a real action;
    - army moves happen in hero-to-hero interaction mode;
    - retreat and surrender are made by BattleAI, not us.
  - **Engine questions:** answered from campaign data. Secondary skills may need more than a week of planning.
  - **Teleports:** Dimension Door and Town Portal are the relevant cases.
  - **Turn length:** a price per decision, with a cap of 256.
  - **Maps:** random maps with more than 2 players; water and underground stay on.
  - **Data:** create data efficiently.
  - **NK2:** do not use it as a teacher. Turn its calculations into in-graph ONNX features (e.g. P(can kill stack)).
  - **Build instruction:** "start working, do not run simulations/plays that take over 3 minutes (this is a build stage, not a science preformance testing stage). I want my AI to play against itself for a 5 round game and do a single step. build it now."

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- VCMI 1.7.5 with Nullkiller2 (NK2) and BattleAI.
- AlphaMapAI (C++ campaign AI) and AlphaBattleAI (C++ battle AI).
- Inference server over Unix sockets ("battle" and "campaign" families); RecordServer.

- Battle credit: the linear round window (no decay). Campaign credit: Monte Carlo on shares at d+2/d+5/d+7, flat mean, minus decision price and old value. There is no exponent anywhere: verified by grep; battle γ=1, λ only used by the unused "gae" option.
- Gradient coherence (‖mean g‖/mean‖g_i‖ against the 1/√K noise level) and the t-statistic of each loss term's effect on log p(SHOOT), through Adam preconditioning (exp_avg_sq).
- Adam in the noise-dominated regime: LR, batch size and betas don't change the ratio of a consistent bias to a consistent signal.
- ActionScore pattern (battle model): pointer score + linear + tiny + wide layers.
- NK2 RewardEvaluator:
  - getGoldReward: resource 600 (gold) / 100 (other); chest 1500; water wheel 1000; mine 5×(1000 gold / 75 other); Pandora 2500; prison 2500.
  - getArmyReward: Pandora 5000; spell scroll 1500; artifacts via potential score; dwellings via AI value × count.
  - getSkillReward: Library 8, Arena 2, scholar-type objects 1, shrines 0.25 / 1 / 2.
  - Strategic value: mine 1 + requirement strength; Keymaster 0.6.
  - Gather score = strategic×1000 + gold (÷2 if >500 else ×2) + army (÷10 for scouts).
- VCMI visited semantics:
  - CRewardableObject::wasVisited(PlayerColor) uses only that player's own visitedObjects, so it matches player knowledge.
  - wasVisited(hero) covers per-hero visits.
  - wasVisitedBefore uses hidden information; we do not use it.

  - New helper:
    ```python
    def entropy_term(out, config=PPOConfig(), weight=None):
        h = entropy(out); bonus = weighted_mean(h, weight)
        term = -config.entropy_weight * bonus
        if config.entropy_floor_weight:
            term = term + config.entropy_floor_weight * weighted_mean(torch.relu(config.entropy_floor - h), weight)
        return term, bonus, (h < config.entropy_floor).float().mean().detach()
    ```
  - ppo_loss and search_loss now use `total = policy + config.value_weight * value + entropy_loss`, and their parts include "below_floor".

  - in-graph: scatter objects to the grid (sum + max pooling), a conv pyramid 36→18→9, hero tokens attend over coarse cells with relative positions, global token gets a pooled summary.

- **Representation fixes:** scales(x, (2,8,32)) for primary skills and level; log(1+0.05·attack/defense); move fraction of the day; mana fraction plus scales.
- **Type-head summary features**, trainable from init 0.
- **Credit option** "mc" (default) or "window" (TD over TURN decisions only, skipping dialog states, plus an outcome share).
- **Campaign learner updates** in a separate spawned process (portable to CUDA).
- **Paired evals:** vs the previous eval on the same setups, mean difference ± SE.

  ```python
  def value_logit(gold): return OBJECT_BASE*torch.tanh(gold/BASE_GOLD) + torch.sign(gold)*torch.log1p(gold.abs()/OBJECT_SCALE)  # 2, 300, 1565
  def offer(logits, legal): return torch.relu(logits).masked_fill(~legal, 0.0).flatten(1).amax(dim=1)  # best entry, never a sum
  def bounded(raw, low, high, start, step): offset=math.log((start-low)/(high-start)); return low+(high-low)*torch.sigmoid(step*raw+offset)
  def fight_return(win, loss, our_army, our_hero, gain): return win*(gain - loss*our_army) - (1-win)*(our_army + our_hero)
  ```

    ```python
    class ChainedHeads(nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, dim, n):
            self.first = zero_output(nn.Linear(dim, 1))
            self.corrections = nn.ModuleList(zero_output(nn.Linear(dim, 1)) for _ in range(n - 1))
        def forward(self, hidden):
            logits = [self.first(hidden).squeeze(-1)]
            for correction in self.corrections:
                logits.append(logits[-1].detach() + correction(hidden).squeeze(-1))
            return torch.stack(logits, dim=-1)
    ```
- `value_hidden` + `value_heads` replace value_head.

```python
		for rows, inputs in _prefetched(chunks, decode, device):
			index = torch.from_numpy(rows).to(device)
			logp, _ = chosen_logp(model(inputs), {k: choice[k][index] for k in ("kind", "action", "index", "cell")})
			anchor[index] = logp
```

```python
full = [bool(t["m_type"][i, ActionType.SHOOT]) and full_shot(t, i) for i in range(len(r.actions))]

moves = [i for i in chances if r.actions[i]["type"] == ActionType.MOVE]
escaped = dict(zip(moves, melee_escapes(t, moves, [r.actions[i] for i in moves]))) if moves else {}
for i in chances:
    kind = r.actions[i]["type"]
    if kind == ActionType.WAIT:
        later = next((j for j in range(i + 1, len(r.actions)) if int(t["g_round"][j]) == int(t["g_round"][i]) and int(t["g_active_unit"][j]) == int(t["g_active_unit"][i])), None)
        wasted = later is not None and not full[later]
    else:
        wasted = kind == ActionType.DEFEND or (kind == ActionType.MOVE and not escaped[i])
```

2. Key Technical Concepts

  - Fixed-point ×2^20: input `mem`, output `memory`.
  - Read before think, through a zero-init residual; write after think_again (GRU-like gate).
  - Only TURN decisions write it (AlphaMapAI).
  - PPO uses the recorded mem as an input, so the writer is not trained in RL (BPTT in PPO is deferred).
- Teacher recorder: AlphaTeacherAI (Teacher.cpp).

- **NK2 advisor (DAgger), Advice.cpp:**

  - RECORD_ADVICE (flag 4) adds 3 int32 after the record header; `records.Decision.advice`.
  - teacher_ce loss on advised decisions at teacher_weight; teacher_mix "score": share = clip(1.2−score, 0.05, 0.95), weight = share/(1−share), initially 19.
- Credit "schedule": w × (real Δlogit7 − V7 forecast) + (1−w) × (real growth7 − G7), w = min(1, 0.1 + 0.02·day). share_target "change" (labels.share_change_labels, cap ±3).

```cpp
void AlphaMapAI::exchange(ObjectInstanceID first, ObjectInstanceID second, QueryID queryID)
{
	futile.clear();
	for(int n = 0; n < 32 && !stopping; ++n)
	{
		auto decision = encode(DecisionKind::EXCHANGE, first, second);
		if(!decision.inputs.any(In::army_mask)) break;
		const auto choice = decide(decision);
		if(choice.type != static_cast<int>(ActionType::ARMY)) break;
		if(!execute(decision, choice)) { noEffect(choice); futile.insert({choice.type, choice.index, choice.cell}); }
		else futile.clear();
	}
	futile.clear();
	answer(queryID, 0);
}
```
joint7 is running on the PC (unit alpha-joint7, resumed 23:51 with the permit fix, 18 VCMI games). The Mac worker serves campaign inference.

- No GitHub Actions.
- Commits end with "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>".
- decide-dont-defer: decide clear calls, recommend on balanced ones, ping on major ones.

**Key user directives this session:**
- "just make sure that we can't die and lose data, if we do you failed. under this constraint, be as fast as possible." Losing partial Mac games when the Mac goes offline doesn't count. The PC must work alone.
- Check the run every hour; after 3 hours up, every 2 hours. Now on the 2-hourly cron job 093bc0b6 ("24 */2 * * *").
- Latest instructions:
  - Value-head normalization "with a tiny stability thingy", without exploding the optimizer.
  - NK2's current goal should be "an inner head like the other ones", not a V that affects policy. The user is unsure how to formulate it.
  - "reward per movement cost" should be an op (no new features) with a few trainable weights, so that ignoring the feature gives the same score. Either a logit change or a qkv scheme.
  - Battle: "keep roughly a fixed ratio. Most battles should be from the campaign … maybe 60:20:20? figure it out." Earlier: 90% fewer battles, same batch size and LR.

- Questions still unanswered: "AlphaStar's statistic z => how is this different from our LSTM state?", and the formulation of the NK2 goal head.

2. Key Technical Concepts

- **PC setup:** RTX 5090 in WSL2, 28 GB RAM and 8 GB swap. Run unit `alpha-joint7` starts `runtime/advice_run.sh`.
- **Keeper:** `alpha-joint7-keeper` runs `runtime/keeper.sh`.
- **Guard (`memwatch.sh`):** stops the run only when available memory is under 0.5 GB **and** free swap is under 2 GB; shedding is off.
- **Deploys:** always `DEPLOY="<script>" runtime/drain_restart.sh`, run from the Mac. It waits for games to finish (≤45 min), uses `pgrep -x vcmiclient` for the Mac, then restarts.
- **Spool:** finished games are written to `<run>/campaign/finished/*.pkl` and reloaded on resume. A waiting game's inputs are dropped from memory (`strip_inputs`); the labeler reads the spool path; `restore_inputs` brings them back when an update takes the game.
- **Battle buffer:** saved at stop and reloaded on resume.
- **Memory admission:** available − (trainer's 20-min peak − its RSS) ≥ 4.5 GB. `LAST_GAME_GB` 3.5 lets one game of each kind start regardless. Remote slots wait only for `LAST_GAME_GB`.
- **Eval games** get memory priority via `eval_waiting`.
- **Permits** are recounted every 10 s.
- **Memory census:** "trainer memory:" log line every 2 min.
- **Other memory fixes:** pinned host memory freed after each update; the trainer trims after each update; the battle batch is densified on the GPU (`dense_on`); the battle eval reduces records as it plays them (`play_eval` reduce).
- **Teacher weight** comes from the last eval's score on resume.
- **TensorBoard:** `alpha-tb` on port 6008, with `--reload_multifile=true`. The Mac tunnel is `ssh -L 6008:172.21.77.108:6008`.
- **Value heads:** V2/V5/V7 (share change, `SHARE_CHANGE_CAP` 3), G2/G5/G7 (growth, `GROWTH_CAP` ln 10), V_end. All use BCE on 0..1 targets.
- **Measured target std** on the 0..1 scale: V2 0.010, V5 0.015, V7 0.017, V_end 0.097. V_end's constant-prediction loss is 35–100× each share head's.
- **Hires vs heroes:** self-play players make 5.6 hire decisions per game but keep only 2.86 heroes. In evals where we survive, we keep 1.45 heroes vs NK2's 4.3. So the gap is hero survival, not hiring.
- **Campaign eval** samples at `eval_temperature` 1.0; battle evals are greedy.
- **NK2 target scoring:** `CaptureObjectsBehavior` scores every object, using `closestWayRatio` and `PriorityEvaluator`. Its movement discount is 1/(0.75 + days^1.3) (`ft.movement_discount`); our `travel_prior` already uses it for TRAVEL, but the MOVE prior did not.

2. Key Technical Concepts

- **PC run joint7:** RTX 5090 under WSL2, 27 GB RAM, unit `alpha-joint7` (runtime/advice_run.sh), keeper, memwatch guard (stops when available < 0.5 GB AND swap free < 2 GB).
- **Deploy pattern:** `DEPLOY=script runtime/drain_restart.sh 60`.
  - The DEPLOY script copies files to the PC via `git archive <sha> files | ssh ... 'wsl --cd ~/projects/alpha_heroes tar xf -'`. The PC's git HEAD stays old.
  - C++ builds on the PC use `nice -n 19 ninja -j4 <targets>` (never -j24: that caused a memory-guard stop).
- **Mac worker:**
  - Runs from worktree `~/projects/alpha_heroes-e5` (branch nk2-advice; `git merge --ff-only main`), started with `(SLOTS=4 nohup runtime/mac_slots.sh alpha-joint7 campaign.inference_processes=2 > /dev/null 2>&1 &)`.
  - Its binaries are e5/build → build-own: an APFS clone with rpaths rewritten and ad-hoc signed, refreshed via `runtime/mac_worker_build.sh` at each C++ deploy.
  - Stop it before a PC restart when the state dict or record format changes.
- **Process matching:** use `[x]yz` bracket patterns or PIDs with pgrep/pkill. A literal pattern matches my own shell's command line.
- **Loss shares (GradNorm closed form):**
  - weight_i = s_i/s_policy × exp(E_policy − E_i) over EMA log gradient norms, ≤ ×1.25 per update.
  - Teacher share = min(0.6, clip(1.2 − score, 0.05, 0.95)); the rest is policy : value : foresight = 4:3:1.
  - kl_target = target_kl × (1 + s_t/s_p).
- **Teacher over the turn:** a pending-set cross-entropy, −log Σ p over the advice entry, RECORD_ACCEPT entries and pending town items.
- **NK2 plan head:** foresight part "plan" with zero-init outputs; `plan_outputs` is set only on the trainer's model.
- **Measurements:**
  - Campaign update ~4.5 min, ~11-12 updates/h. Games now outrun the learner (backpressure via Supply.allow permits: "while the games finished and in flight hold less than `ahead` updates' worth of decisions").
  - CUDA graphs: Graphs/StepGraph with fixed shapes; StepGraph keyed by `signature(inputs, batch)`, the tensor shapes and dtypes.
  - model_v2 windows: fixed_windows on CUDA computes all B×8 hero-slot windows; a player averages ~2.5 heroes.

  ```python
  ADDED = ("features.move_discount",)

  def with_added(state_dict, own):
      missing = {key: torch.zeros_like(own[key]) for key in ADDED if key in own and key not in state_dict}
      missing.update({key: value.clone() for key, value in own.items() if key.startswith(ADDED_HEADS) and key not in state_dict})
      return {**state_dict, **missing} if missing else state_dict
  ```

  ```python
  def windows_of(x, fixed=False):
      present = x["h_present"][:, :HO].reshape(-1)
      if fixed:
          return torch.arange(present.shape[0], device=present.device), present.shape[0], present
      windows = torch.nonzero(present).squeeze(-1)
      n = windows.shape[0]
      if n > 0 and windows.device.type == "mps" and not torch.onnx.is_in_onnx_export():
          windows = torch.cat([windows, windows[:1].expand((1 << (n - 1).bit_length()) - n)])
      return windows, n, torch.arange(windows.shape[0], device=windows.device) < n
  ```
  - `dense(values, windows, n, b)` (model.py:156): `values.new_zeros((b*HO,)+shape).index_copy(0, windows[:n], values[:n]).reshape((b,HO)+shape)`.

**campaign_model/advice.py (new):**

**campaign_model/train.py:**

- The micro_loss set CE:
  ```python
  if "accept_types" in batch:
      advised_own = torch.where(batch["advice_has"], advice_logp.clamp(min=-1e4), torch.full_like(advice_logp, -1e4)).unsqueeze(1)
      advice_logp = torch.logsumexp(torch.cat([advised_own, entries_logp(out, batch["accept_types"], batch["accept_index"], batch["accept_valid"])], dim=1), dim=1)
      advised = advised * (advice_logp > -1e3).float()
  ```
- Loss weights come in as `batch.get("lw:value",1.0)` and `batch.get("lw:foresight",1.0)`. `split=True` returns raw terms (policy, value, entropy, teacher, foresight, plan).
- ppo_update gains `teacher_sets=None, loss_weights=None`; sets `model.plan_outputs = True` when foresight labels are present; the gradient check uses `prefix="grad_raw_"`.

**campaign_model/learner.py:**

4. Errors and fixes

- **Mac games lost (~8).** e5/build was a symlink to the main build; my Mac C++ build changed the worker's binaries while its Python couldn't parse the new records. Fixed by giving the worker its own build-own clone with rewritten rpaths (8759589).
- **Memory-guard stop at 11:22.** The PC build ran at -j24 during a drain. No data was lost. PC builds now use nice -j4.
- **pkill self-match.** A `pkill -f "ssh -N .*-L 7800:"` killed my own shell. Now I use bracket patterns.
- **The old mac_slots.sh ignored TERM.** Fixed with kill -9 on its PIDs.
- **Python name collision.** `own` in micro_loss (the value loss vs the advice logp) produced a stack shape error; renamed the advice one to `advised_own`.
- **Plan head started too loud.** At random init its CE was 14.5 nats and its shared gradient 2× the policy's. Fixed with zero_output on plan_out and plan_none.
- **Test fixes:** every-part-learns needed `plan_outputs`; a random linear loss for the zero-init fs_plan; the learner tests now bind loss_shares, loss_weights and _note_gradients; `grad_raw_` naming.
- **Quoting and scripting mistakes:**

  - a zsh glob abort (ran under bash -c);
  - a Windows-ssh `bash -c` quoting failure (used the single-outer/double-inner form);
  - `tr "\0"` lost through ssh, which gave false "0 training games" readings (count_games.sh now uses `grep -a`).
- **psutil missing.** Measured COW memory via /proc smaps_rollup on the PC instead.
- **Wrong claim to the user.** I said the advice labels NK2's destination; it's actually the chain's first node. Corrected to the user.
- **Over-estimate of the preload fix.** 1.4 GB was expected; ~0.2 GB was real. Told the user honestly.
- **Memory rule change.** I set a 4 GB revert rule for 11 games. Min memory hit 3.97, 3.96 and 3.69 GB during eval overlaps; I kept 11 with a new 3 GB revert threshold, explained it to the user, and offered to revert.

Starting the window budget for the CUDA trainer: the user said "Try to get more speed for the same model". After the 05:26 check I told the user: "I'll start on the real-heroes window change next, since it's what you asked for (more speed, same model). Before deploying it I'll check the outputs match the current code exactly."

I had read:
- windows_of (fixed → all B×HO slots);
- dense (index_copy of the first n windows);
- all model_v2 uses of known.windows (index-based; compatible with any K);
- graphs.StepGraph, which keys graphs by `signature(inputs, batch)` (shapes and dtypes), records once, and is checked against eager with TOLERANCE 1e-3.

Design so far:
- windows_of budget mode: `order = torch.argsort((~present).to(torch.int8), stable=True); windows = order[:K]; real = present[windows]; n = K`.
- dense places all K: padding windows are distinct absent slots zeroed by real_only, so output is identical to the fixed path.
- Overflow (real heroes > K) must be decided on the host, before graph replay. Plan: compute per-row hero counts in the prepass on the GPU, one `.cpu()` at the end. Then per micro-batch: budget path if `counts[rows].sum() ≤ K`, else the full fixed or eager path.
- The budget must be part of the StepGraph signature, e.g. a batch tensor whose shape encodes K, or separate StepGraph instances per mode.
- Testing CUDA on the live PC is risky (a past CUDA test caused a memory-guard stop). Instead: verify on CPU that the budget-path outputs are bit-identical to the fixed path, then rely on StepGraph's built-in first-replay check on the PC.

4. Write a CPU test that budget-mode outputs equal fixed-mode outputs exactly.
5. Run a Mac smoke, then deploy with a drain (Python only) and compare update seconds before and after. The current update breakdown is ~250 s: prepass ~36-42, decode waits ~24-33, "launching forward" ~177.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

**The run and its deploy mechanics**
- joint7 runs on the PC (RTX 5090, WSL, ssh `Owner@192.168.1.178` → `wsl`). Unit `alpha-joint7` plus a keeper. The Mac worker runs from worktree `~/projects/alpha_heroes-e5`.
- Deploy pattern: `DEPLOY="bash <script>" runtime/drain_restart.sh 60`.
  - drain_restart drains, runs DEPLOY with no game playing, then stops and restarts the PC unit. **It ignores DEPLOY's exit code.**
  - DEPLOY scripts copy files via `git archive <sha> files | ssh ... 'wsl --cd ~/projects/alpha_heroes tar xf -'` and stop the Mac worker.
  - The all-script then ff-merges e5 and restarts the Mac worker with `(SLOTS=9 nohup runtime/mac_slots.sh alpha-joint7 campaign.inference_processes=6 ...)`.
  - Verify the PC's files with md5 against the last deployed commit before every deploy.
- Monitors: `watch_joint7.sh` and the PC memory monitor (<4 GB: back to 8 games and 8 servers), re-armed every 30 minutes. Cron 2-hourly checks.
- Campaign config on the PC:
  - batch_size 8192, passes 3, ~18 optimizer steps per update, ~300 steps per hour, lr 1e-5.
  - Teacher (NK2 advice) share 60%.

**Model (v2)**
- Window W=33 (radius 16), HO=8, d=128, dc=16, dp=64.
- pairs = pair_hero + pair_tile + pair_reach + pair_fight + pair_strength.
- Foresight SwiGLU.
- move_pair SwiGLU(64+16+27+16 → 64).

**MOVE features**
- 27 known features: 10 kept MOVE_FEATURES, 8 strength, 6 relative, 3 foreseen (detached).
- Mixer (`move_mix*`): 27→16→1 plus a direct path initialized at hopeless × −3.
- Prior correction `safety` (6 × hopeless, bounded 0..12).

**Strength estimates**
- army: count × fight value.
- total: NK2's estimate.
- race: VCMI damage modifiers (+5% per attack point to +300%, −2.5% per point to −70%; HP × damage, sqrt).
- spells: the race plus the best damage spell. Spell table: ids 15–23, base + power × coef, mana cost, up to 3 casts over 3 rounds.
- For this hero and our strongest hero.
- Smooth `hopeless` (softplus at temperature 0.25, tanh bound) and `strength_view` (2·tanh(r/2)).

**Foresight**
- 3-class tiles head: no monster / attacks / offers. Engine answer only; "offers" means a YES_NO dialog with d_object 54 in the window.
- Outcome head: P(win), stake_lost.

**Trades**
- VCMI market rates: efficiency min((markets+1)/20, 0.5); give/get per IMarket::getOffer; amounts as the Encoder makes them.
- trade_options gives the cost and the unlock flag (building short of exactly one resource, or a recruit we can't afford one of).
- Prior: +2 for an unlock, −0.5 × log(1 + cost/1000).

**Schema 11**
- o_battle and o_join per option.
- d_guard_creature/count/detailed.
- a_present/a_pos/a_creature/a_count/a_detailed for 32 garrisons.
- Old records zero-padded via SHORT_RECORDS.

**VCMI facts**
- The pathfinder never passes through guarded tiles.
- CGGarrison: battle immediately on visit; army visible via right-click (quantity ids).
- CGMine guarded: asks a YES/NO question; no components.
- Rewardable banks: guards hidden until scouted; the question shows exact guards (showGuardsComposition=true).
- CGCreature dialogs:

| Dialog | Text / components | YES | NO |
|---|---|---|---|
| Join for free | ADVOB 86 | join | battle |
| Join for gold | advobtxt[90] + a gold RESOURCE component | join | battle |
| Flee | ADVOB 91 | pursue (battle) | lets it go |

**Checkpoints**
- Atomic saves (`save_atomically`).
- `fit_moments` remaps Adam moments when shapes change.

- New smooth views:
  ```python
  def hopeless(ratio):
      ramp = 0.25 * F.softplus((-ratio - 1.0) / 0.25)
      return 6.0 * torch.tanh(ramp / 6.0) / 3.0
  def strength_view(ratio):
      return 2.0 * torch.tanh(ratio / 2.0)
  ```

  ```python
  self.option_flags = zero_output(nn.Linear(2, d))
  self.answer_fight, self.answer_join = zero_output(nn.Linear(6, 1)), zero_output(nn.Linear(3, 1))
  state.options = self.encode_options(x) + self.option_flags(torch.stack([x["o_battle"], x["o_join"]], dim=-1).float()) * x["option_mask"].unsqueeze(-1)
  answers = x["o_battle"].float() * (q["battle_prior"] + self.answer_fight(q["fight"]).squeeze(-1)).unsqueeze(1) + x["o_join"].float() * (q["join_prior"] + self.answer_join(q["join"]).squeeze(-1)).unsqueeze(1)
  ```

4. Errors and fixes:
- **FIGHT_STEP proposal:** it conflicted with the user's LR rule, so I didn't deploy it and it was later dropped. Once the actual gradient efficiency was measured, my "1.6 logits/hour" estimate turned out to be only a ceiling; I corrected it to ~0.06/h.
- **Test copy lacked vocab.json:** every comparison looked broken (huge KL) until I symlinked `battle_model/artifacts/vocab.json`.
- **Torn checkpoint copy:** a copy of checkpoint_latest.pt taken mid-write was corrupt. This revealed non-atomic saves, fixed with save_atomically.
- **Wrong path-interception claim:** I told the user ~20% of suicides came from paths crossing guard zones. That was wrong. VCMI's pathfinder forbids it; the cases were garrisons, towns and a mine. I corrected it to the user.
- **The v832 eval dip (0.387):** I judged it noise from elimination flips, and v854 recovered to 0.444.
- **C++ helpers in an anonymous namespace:** seenCount and topObject were not visible from AlphaMapAI.cpp, so I moved encodeQuestionGuards and fromTemplate into Encoder.cpp.
- **spell_damage built tensors from Python lists on the device:** this breaks CUDA graph capture. Fixed with the cached `constant()`.
- Other fixes:
  - A tests update (the move_win key in hand-built label dicts).
  - The gradient test (fs_tiles/fs_outcome start at 0, so they go in the random-weighted sum).

- **The user rejected "boolean" hopeless:** made smooth.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   **The run (joint7)**
   - Runs on the PC (RTX 5090, WSL, ssh Owner@192.168.1.178 → wsl, ~/projects/alpha_heroes), unit alpha-joint7 plus a keeper.

     - links on the PC in the drain window, checking the hash with `grep -aq`, with a rollback;

     - check_s11.py: resume, load_optimizer, an Adam step on CUDA, ppo_update graphs vs eager.
     - check_infer.py: GPU InferenceServer graph capture.
     - same_policy.py: old vs new outputs on 300 real decisions.

   **Contracts**

   | Schema | Commit | Hash | Note |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | 11 | c01038f | 3653f6c54ad2233b | |
   | 12 | 542fdbe | 6ee1ae97fd6670c0 | resource_worth output; inputs as 11 |
   | 13 | 87e79b5, not deployed | de82f54cc368f0b3 | ARTIFACT action, artifact_mask and x_heroes inputs, artifact_logp output; IN_BYTES 878990; SHORT_RECORDS {873973, 876021, 878334} |

   **Question head**
   - Option identity is built from tied tables: hero skill rows, artifact rows, spell columns, army creature rows, global object/pair rows.
   - YES uses the asked object (not our hero); flagged options hold the guards' army.
   - Components get type, primary and resource rows.
   - The key is the raw identity plus a bias-free numbers projection, plus a zero-init option_context.
   - logit = atanh(0.995·cos(decision, key)) + option_prior + option_weights(option_features).

   **Trade purchases**
   - tradesFor greedily buys each short resource with whole lots of the spare resource that loses the least worth per unit.
   - The worth comes from the model's resource_worth (VCMI prices); feasibility is tested at the market's own sale prices.
   - payByTrades runs before the purchase.
   - features.purchase_trades prices it in the BUILD/RECRUIT priors at the trade cost weight.

   **Φ army multiplier**
   - sqrt((1+0.05·min(A,60))/(1−0.025·min(D,28))) applied to the hero's army; garrisons count ×1.

   **VCMI facts**
   - Artifact positions: worn 0..18, BACKPACK_START 19.
   - Boats: BuildingID::SHIP = 20, IShipyard::shipyardStatus()==IBoatGenerator::GOOD.
   - University: MARKETS_UNIVERSITY_GOLD_COST; buy with cb->trade(..., RESOURCE_SKILL, GOLD, skill, 1, hero).
   - Market: cb->trade gives whole lots; getOffer(id1, id2, give, get, mode).
   - cb->waitTillRealize=true, so requests are synchronous.
   - NK2's yes/no: YES by default; NO for a dangerous object that isn't its goal, or a guarded artifact/resource with unknown or too-high danger.
   - NK2 selection: the last component, except a chest's gold for a scout or under gold pressure.

   - Hero factor = U × m, with m = √((1+0.05A)/(1−0.025D)) × (1+0.02√(SP×K)) when the hero has a spellbook.

   - The eval keeps the old Φ (EVAL_EMPIRE, spell_step 0); training's Φ is version 4, the eval's version 3.
   - MOVE meeting value: the weaker hero's army × the factor difference; a town tile is worth its garrison × (the hero's factor − U); learned weight meeting_gain.

   **Schema 14 MULTI (ActionType 13), MultiKind:**
   - RECRUIT_ALL, RECRUIT_ALL_HERO, GATHER (all per town);

3. Files and Code Sections:

**game_values/__init__.py** — the shared Φ constants.
- DAMAGE_SPELLS and SPELL_ROUNDS moved here. SPELL_STEP=0.02, REFERENCE_POWER, REFERENCE_KNOWLEDGE = 3, 2.
- Code:
  ```python
  def spell_factor(power, knowledge, spellbook, step=SPELL_STEP):
  	return 1.0 + step * math.sqrt(max(power, 0) * max(knowledge, 0)) if spellbook else 1.0
  def army_multiplier(attack, defense, power=0, knowledge=0, spellbook=False, step=SPELL_STEP):
  	dealt = 1.0 + ATTACK_STEP * min(max(attack, 0), ATTACK_POINTS)
  	taken = 1.0 - DEFENSE_STEP * min(max(defense, 0), DEFENSE_POINTS)
  	return math.sqrt(dealt / taken) * spell_factor(power, knowledge, spellbook, step)
  def unled_share(step=SPELL_STEP):
  	return 1.0 / spell_factor(REFERENCE_POWER, REFERENCE_KNOWLEDGE, True, step)
  ```

**campaign_model/empire.py**
- EmpireConfig gains `spell_step: float = SPELL_STEP`. `EVAL_EMPIRE = EmpireConfig(spell_step=0.0)`; EMPIRE_VERSION = 4, EVAL_EMPIRE_VERSION = 3.
- `hero_multiplier(hero, config)` = unled_share × army_multiplier(attack, defense, power, knowledge, spellbook from "spellbook" or artifact 0).
- Army part: `unled_share(step) * army_worth(stacks) + Σ hero armies × hero_multiplier`.

  ```python
  pair = x["x_heroes"] - 1
  receivers = torch.gather(state.heroes, 1, pair.clamp(min=0).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, d)) * (pair >= 0).unsqueeze(-1).float()
  receivers = torch.cat([receivers, receivers.new_zeros(b, TO - receivers.shape[1], d)], dim=1)
  by_town = (self.multi_q(own_towns) @ kinds.t()) / math.sqrt(d); by_receiver = (self.multi_q(receivers) @ kinds.t()) / math.sqrt(d)
  ```

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   **Φ v5 (training; the eval keeps v3 via EVAL_EMPIRE)**
   - A hero's army = worth × U × m(hero) × (movementPointsLand[slowest speed]/2000)^0.25 + make-up morale value.
   - Make-up morale: VCMI's rule, one faction +1, n factions 2−n, any undead −1. Priced at 2% of the living army per 24th of battle chance (good [1,2,3]/24, bad [2,4,6]/24), within caps, over the hero's other morale (h_morale includes the make-up).

   **Schema 15 MULTI**
   - Kinds: RECRUIT_ALL 0, RECRUIT_ALL_HERO 1, GATHER 2, GIVE_ALL 3, TAKE_BEST 4, TAKE_BEST_FAST 5, DROP_SLOW 6, DROP_FOREIGN 7. N_MULTI 8. multi_mask is (TO=8, 8).
   - Rows: towns for the recruit and gather kinds; the exchange receiver (0 = x_heroes' first) for GIVE_ALL / TAKE_BEST / TAKE_BEST_FAST; the hero token for the DROP kinds.

   **Priors**
   - GIVE_ALL, TAKE_BEST and TAKE_BEST_FAST toward the weaker hero pay give_all_cost (3). The stronger hero: higher level, then Φ factor, then army.
   - GATHER: gain minus gather_loss (emptied_danger × buildings − town_danger × stakes, weight gather_risk 1). A scout's GATHER pays gather_cost (3).
   - Teacher sets drop GATHER (advice.taught); accepted entries ARE taught (rich-get-richer).
   - The teacher accepts TAKE_BEST toward NK2's destination.

   **Engine mechanics**
   - unifyStacks() merges duplicate stacks before every turn decision, so SPLIT_SINGLES is deferred and MERGE_ALL is redundant.
   - VCMI bulkMoveArmy: source slots in order; merge, else a free slot, else the stack stays; if all would move, one creature stays.
   - mirrored in Python by features.bulk_moves.
   - NK2's getBestArmy (faction-greedy, morale-weighted power), ported to Armies.cpp because eval games have no advisor.

   **Records and checkpoints**

   - real_share 0.6, real_keep 0.12, league 0.5,0.5,0.0, eval_minutes 180.

     ```python
     prior = self.multi_weight_value() * ft.value_logit(self.multi_gain(x, f))
     cost = torch.zeros_like(prior)
     weaker = (1.0 - self.stronger_receiver(x, f)) * self.give_all_cost_weight()
     for kind in (c.MultiKind.GIVE_ALL, c.MultiKind.TAKE_BEST, c.MultiKind.TAKE_BEST_FAST):
         cost[:, :2, kind] = weaker
     cost[..., c.MultiKind.GATHER] = (1.0 - self.main_visitor(x, f)) * self.gather_cost_weight()
     return prior - cost
     ```

2. Key Technical Concepts:

**Battle learner (8797125)**
- accumulate 2 (48 Adam steps/update) at lr 1e-5; beta2 0.99.
- outcome_share = outcome_share_max = 0.5 (fixed).
- lopsided_scale = 1/(4p(1−p)), with p capped at LOPSIDED_MIN_SHARE 0.02 (≤×12.8).
- Shot type features full_shot / half_shot_only. DEFEND×full_shot prior: −2, later −5. Load hooks widen old checkpoints.
- Shared.credit passes the trainer's outcome share and policy heads to the pool workers.

**Inference load balancing (7a7da98)**
- Shared.server_games; Games.take_server / release_server (least-loaded).
- The battle and campaign models share the same 12 processes.

**Teacher span (train.TEACHER_SPAN)**
- The teacher CE is normalized within the label's span:
  - hero label (MOVE/TRAVEL/ARMY, CAST) → hero + END + TRADE + MULTI;
  - town label / END → hero + town + END (+TRADE, MULTI);
  - exchange → ARMY + END;
  - accepted entries are added.
- Unmatched NK2 requests get no teacher loss. teacher_outside is logged.

**Loss balancer (loss_shares, GradNorm-style)**
- The value share is now a floor (VALUE_SHARE_CAP 3).

**DROP prior**
- share = gain / hero's led value, clamped to [−1, 1].
- prior = drop_scale(20) × share + disbanded × (drop_sign(6) × sign(share) − disband_cost(6)).

**TRADE**
- trade_waste 5 for trades that unlock no building, creature or hero hire.

**CUDA graphs**
- Python-list indices create CPU tensors that break capture → eager steps 3-4× slower.
- The update line's "losses N, backward N" nonzero means eager.

**Rollback procedure**
- Quarantine the checkpoint and waiting games; renumber the old checkpoint (version and games_used from the latest).

**Other mechanisms and measurements**
- Paired mini-eval on the Mac: scratchpad mini_eval.py with BattleAI battles.
- type_drift.py: two checkpoints on the same decisions.
- Stall diagnosis: headless.sh writes a Linux /proc dump; BattleProcess queue protocol (TestQueue: "ready" → job; gameOver).
- NK2 isSafeToVisit uses safeAttackRatio 1.1. Our hopeless() ramp starts at e×; the safety weight is 6.

**battle_model/features.py**
```python
BATTLE_TYPE_FEATURES = ("danger_here", "threat_here", "strength")
SHOT_TYPE_FEATURES = ("full_shot", "half_shot_only")
TYPE_FEATURES = (*BATTLE_TYPE_FEATURES, *SHOT_TYPE_FEATURES)
def shot_types(b):
	near = (rules.hex_distance(b.me["hex"], b.units["hex"]) <= rules.SHOOTING_PENALTY_DISTANCE) | ((b.units["rear"] >= 0) & (rules.hex_distance(b.me["hex"], b.units["rear"]) <= rules.SHOOTING_PENALTY_DISTANCE))
	legal = b.x["m_shoot_target"] != 0
	full = (legal & (near | b.me["no_distance_penalty"])).any(dim=1)
	return torch.stack([full, legal.any(dim=1) & ~full], dim=-1).float()
```
The "type" action features are `torch.cat([pick(BATTLE_TYPE_FEATURES, ...), shot_types(b)], dim=-1)`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   **The joint7 run**
   - Training on the PC (WSL, RTX 5090) with a Mac worker. The campaign model is v2; the battle model is model v2/v3.
   - Deploys: `runtime/drain_restart.sh` with a DEPLOY hook (deploy_NN.sh), plus an aNN_all.sh wrapper. The wrapper checks the PC files, fast-forwards the e5 worktree and restarts the Mac worker (`SLOTS=9 nohup runtime/mac_slots.sh alpha-joint7 campaign.inference_processes=6`).
   - CUDA graph capture check: scratchpad capture_check.py on the stopped run's GPU.
   - Rollback procedure: stop the run, quarantine checkpoint_latest and finished games, copy checkpoint_<v>.pt as latest renumbered to the slid version and games_used, restart. The Mac worker must be stopped first, because its servers hold the weights under the same version.

   **Teacher weight**
   - `teacher_weight_for(score) = λ/(1−λ)`, λ = clip(1.2 − score, 0.05, 0.95).
   - It is noisy: 2.62 at 0.476, 9.33 at 0.297.

   **Value targets**
   - share_target "change": the logit change of our share, real outcomes only.
   - Growth and end heads use `ladder_targets` and `end_targets`, bootstrapped from day_starts with per-head trust = head_ev / trust_full_ev (0.4).
   - Config: outcome_share_min 0.25, growth_weight 0.5, GROWTH_CAP log 10, SHARE_CHANGE_CAP 3.
   - fresh value loss = excess cross-entropy averaged over heads.

   **Φ**
   - Φ v4/v5: army worth × U(0.953) × m(hero), speed factor, morale.
   - EVAL_EMPIRE = EmpireConfig(spell_step=0, speed_power=0, army_morale=0).
   - The in-graph Φ (features.empire_value) mirrors the ledger's.

   **VCMI facts**
   - `getTownsInfo(false)` walks only players' towns.

     ```python
     def window_worst(values, monster, default):
         big = 1e9
         low = torch.where(monster, values, torch.full_like(values, big)).flatten(1).amin(dim=1)
         return torch.where(low < big / 2, low, torch.full_like(low, default))[:, None, None].expand_as(values)
     ```

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Adam per-group design.** Named groups with `param_names` in checkpoints; moments matched by name on load; the config's hyperparameters win over the checkpoint's.
   - **Two-timescale actor-critic.** Value group at learning_rate × value_lr_factor (10), apart from the KL control's lr_scale.
   - **The KL control ratchet.** Stop at 1.5× target, raise below 0.5× target. Per-update KL barely follows lr (kl_first ≈ 0.00001, no memory floor), so target 0.015 drove lr to its floor. Reverted to target_kl 0.03.
   - **signal_fraction:** (mean min(1, m̂²/v̂) − k)/(1 − k), with k = (1−β1)/(1+β1). It is β1-invariant, unlike signal_ratio.
   - **Per-group clipping.** Each group clipped at its median norm, then everything × grad_clip/median total (avoids Adam's scale jump). Calibrated over the first 200 steps and stored in the checkpoint; `grad_clip_since` triggers recalibration. The trunk is about 88% of the norm.
   - **Head weights by residual** (step 7): slow 0.98 variances for the scale; 20-update ramp.
   - **Trust floor 0.4** with a 0.98 EMA. Loss shares frozen at v1420 (value 353, teacher 2.03, foresight 0.70).
   - **Battle changes:**
     - values group ×10 and per-group clip;
     - trunk β1 0.95;
     - escape_ahead prior: escapes get w_step·tanh(max(V,0)/0.1) + w_scale·max(V,0), learnable from (−3, −3), with V the detached final value head.
   - **Dumb-decision counters:**
     - pickup_passed_end/travel;
     - troops_left, now per mine dialog (garrison x_count at close vs open);
     - exchange_shuffle;
     - battle failure modes.
   - **Privileged critic (asymmetric actor-critic):**
     - Inputs: rivals' value_hidden states from their last decision on an earlier day, plus ledger log Φ and age.
     - Architecture: a pair MLP, masked max and mean pooling, then a zero-init output producing 7 logit corrections.
     - Detached inputs (it learns on top of the critic).
     - Staged: `privileged_critic` trains and logs; `privileged_credit` uses it for the baselines.
   - **TensorBoard data server:** `--samples_per_plugin scalars=0` keeps NO points; use 5000.
   - **Deploy pattern:** `aNN_all.sh` checks the PC's files, runs `DEPLOY="bash deploy_NN.sh" runtime/drain_restart.sh 60`, fast-forwards e5 and restarts the Mac worker.
     - deploy_NN stops the Mac worker and the run, copies files with `git archive`, checks md5, runs update_check.py on the GPU and the tests on the CPU.
     - On failure it restores the old files.

     ```python
     PRIVILEGED_RIVALS = P - 1
     PRIVILEGED_HEADS = 2 * c.N_VALUE_OFFSETS + 1  # V2 V5 V7, G2 G5 G7, V_end
     PRIVILEGED_PAIR = 2
     PRIVILEGED_WIDTH = 128
     class PrivilegedCritic(nn.Module):
         def __init__(self, dim, width=PRIVILEGED_WIDTH):
             super().__init__()
             self.pair = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2 * dim + PRIVILEGED_PAIR, width), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(width, width), nn.GELU())
             self.out = zero_output(nn.Linear(2 * width + dim + 1, PRIVILEGED_HEADS))
         def forward(self, own, rivals, mask, own_phi, rival_phi, age):
             features = torch.stack([(rival_phi - own_phi[:, None]).clamp(-10.0, 10.0), torch.log1p(age.clamp(min=0.0))], dim=-1)
             pair = self.pair(torch.cat([own[:, None, :].expand(-1, rivals.shape[1], -1), rivals, features], dim=-1))
             m = mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
             any_rival = (m.sum(dim=1) > 0).float()
             most = torch.where(m > 0, pair, torch.full_like(pair, -1e4)).amax(dim=1) * any_rival
             mean = (pair * m).sum(dim=1) / m.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1.0)
             return self.out(torch.cat([most, mean, own, m.sum(dim=1) / rivals.shape[1]], dim=-1))
     ```
     - In __init__: `self.privileged = PrivilegedCritic(d)`.
     - In value(): `out["value_hidden"] = hidden`.

   - **Privileged critic (current, 0fe2869):**
     - `x = [value_hidden, masked max over alive rivals' raw value_hidden, logit(true share now)]`.
     - Correction = `direct(x) + read(GELU(dense(x)))` for V2/V5/V7/V_end. dense is 2d+1→32; direct and read are bias-free and zero-init.

       ```python
       slot_rows = torch.cat([holders.unsqueeze(2).expand(b, HD, A, d), holder_stacks], dim=-1)
       src = self.army_src(slot_rows); dst = self.army_dst(holders)
       within, swap_only, default_slot, swap_slot = self.features.army_slot_priors()
       same_army = torch.eye(HD, device=src.device)[None, :, None, :, None]
       learned[A_.ARMY] = self.army_out(src[:, :, :, None, :] + dst[:, None, None, :, :]) + within * same_army
       slots = army_slots(x)
       whole = (torch.arange(c.N_ARMY_AMOUNTS, device=src.device) == c.ArmyAmount.ALL).float()
       prior[A_.ARMY] = self.features.army_prior(...).expand_as(learned[A_.ARMY]) + swap_only * slots.swap_only.float()[..., None] * whole
       offers[A_.ARMY] = best_legal(prior[A_.ARMY], x["army_mask"])
       slot_logits = torch.einsum("bfsk,btuk->bfstu", self.army_slot_q(src), self.army_slot_k(slot_rows)) / math.sqrt(ARMY_SLOT_KEYS)
       slot_logits = slot_logits + default_slot * slots.default.float() + swap_slot * slots.swap.float()
       out["army_slot_logp"] = masked_log_softmax(slot_logits, slots.legal)
       out["army_split_mask"] = slots.fit
       ```

     ```python
     if "army_slot_logp" in out:
         flat = index.clamp(min=0); amount = flat % c.N_ARMY_AMOUNTS
         to = div(flat, N_ARMY_AMOUNTS) % N_HOLDERS; slot = div(flat, N_ARMY_AMOUNTS*N_HOLDERS) % ARMY_SLOTS
         holder = div(flat, N_ARMY_AMOUNTS*N_HOLDERS*ARMY_SLOTS).clamp(max=N_HOLDERS-1)
         row = out["army_slot_logp"][rows, holder, slot, to].clamp(min=-1e4)
         fit = out["army_split_mask"][rows, holder, slot, to]
         part = torch.logsumexp(row.masked_fill(~fit, -1e4), dim=-1)
         taken = row[rows, cell.clamp(0, ARMY_SLOTS-1)] - torch.where(amount != ALL, part, zero)
         moved = turn & (action == ARMY) & (cell >= 0)
         logp = logp + torch.where(moved, taken, zero)
     ```

    ```python
    def movement_counts(decisions, counts):
        streams, game, last = defaultdict(list), defaultdict(int), {}
        for d in decisions:
            if d.color in last and d.day < last[d.color]:
                game[d.color] += 1
            last[d.color] = d.day
            streams[(d.color, game[d.color])].append(d)
        steps = []
        for key, stream in streams.items():
            for p, d in enumerate(stream):
                if d.kind == c.DecisionKind.TURN and d.action_type in (c.ActionType.MOVE, c.ActionType.TRAVEL) and d.index >= 0:
                    nxt = stream[p + 1] if p + 1 < len(stream) and stream[p + 1].day == d.day else None
                    steps.append((key, d, nxt))
        met = defaultdict(set)
        for start in range(0, len(steps), CHUNK):
            part = steps[start : start + CHUNK]
            rows = [d for _, d, _ in part] + [nxt for _, _, nxt in part if nxt is not None]
            x = decode_fields(rows, MOVE_FIELDS)
            row_of = {}
            for i, d in enumerate(rows): row_of.setdefault(id(d), i)
            for key, d, nxt in part:
                b = row_of[id(d)]; move = d.action_type == c.ActionType.MOVE
                h = min(d.index // (W * W) if move else d.index // (L * TG * TG), HO - 1)
                spent = 0
                if nxt is not None:
                    later = row_of[id(nxt)]
                    same = np.flatnonzero(x["h_present"][later, :HO] & (x["h_type"][later, :HO] == x["h_type"][b, h]))
                    if len(same): spent = max(0, int(x["h_move"][b, h]) - int(x["h_move"][later, same[0]]))
                meeting = town = False
                if move:
                    rest = d.index % (W * W)
                    target = np.array([x["h_pos"][b, h, 0] + rest % W - R, x["h_pos"][b, h, 1] + rest // W - R, x["h_pos"][b, h, 2]])
                    ours = x["h_present"][b, :HO] & (x["h_owner"][b, :HO] == c.OWNER_SELF) & (np.arange(HO) != h)
                    there = np.flatnonzero(ours & (x["h_pos"][b, :HO] == target).all(axis=-1))
                    meeting = len(there) > 0
                    towns = x["t_present"][b, : c.OWN_TOWNS] & (x["t_owner"][b, : c.OWN_TOWNS] == c.OWNER_SELF)
                    town = not meeting and bool((towns & (x["t_pos"][b, : c.OWN_TOWNS] == target).all(axis=-1)).any())
                    counts["own_hero_moves"][0] += meeting; counts["own_hero_moves"][1] += 1
                    if meeting:
                        pair = frozenset((int(x["h_type"][b, h]), int(x["h_type"][b, there[0]])))
                        counts["repeat_meetings"][0] += pair in met[(key, d.day)]
                        counts["repeat_meetings"][1] += 1
                        met[(key, d.day)].add(pair)
                for name, hit in (("meeting_movement", meeting), ("town_movement", town)):
                    counts[name][0] += spent if hit else 0
                    counts[name][1] += spent
    ```
  - Measured on 4 local spool games (8,722 decisions, 1.6 s):
    - own_hero_moves 0.487
    - repeat_meetings 0.619
    - meeting_movement 0.214
    - town_movement 0.18

**Battle training:**
- My Nov 5 accumulate 24→2 was wrong and was reverted (accumulate 24, deploy 47).
- e5's correction: signal_ratio/signal_fraction read noise as signal at accumulate 24.
  - Noise floor: Var(m) = 0.2077σ², |m|/√v rms 0.456, median 0.31.
  - The true per-update signal is ~0.5%.
  - My caveat: a persistent signal outgrows noise after ~200 updates, and more than 1000 have passed. So a flat eval means the direction is unhelpful or non-stationary.
- Gradient concentration: an update rests on ~4 games for the value and ~14 for the policy, because long stand-off battles dominate.
- Fix: per-decision length weight 1/(1+(n−1)/scale).
- New instruments:
  - SignalShare: the first-step gradient against a running mean of earlier updates (decay 0.98), raw and applied.
  - games_rested_on.
- PPG-style auxiliary value phase:
  - Replay in ActorPool; its own Adam.
  - Loss: value_loss + kl_weight·KL(snapshot‖policy), with kl_weight 10.
  - Nets: skip non-finite steps; reject when KL exceeds aux_kl_max 3e-4; guarded_run; 16-step warm-up; Adam state saved in checkpoints.

battle_model/aux_value.py:

  - `reset()`, `state_dict()`/`load_state_dict()`;
  - `run(...)`, which makes a snapshot deepcopy, calls `undo()` via `load_state_dict(snapshot.state_dict())`, and rejects when a reading or weight is non-finite or `aux_kl_check > kl_max` (only if `aux_steps > 0`);
  - `_run(...)`, which skips non-finite losses, sets the warm-up lr as `self.rates[name] * min(1, (steps_done + 1) / WARMUP)`, and logs readings `aux_steps`, `aux_skipped`, `aux_rejected`, `aux_value_loss`, `aux_kl`, `aux_grad_cosine`, `aux_trunk_move`, `aux_excess_before`/`after`, `aux_kl_check`, `aux_kl_held_out`, `aux_seconds`.
- `guarded_run(phase, ...)` returns `(readings, phase)` or `({"aux_failed": 1}, None)` and prints "aux: FAILURE: ...".

battle_model/distributions.py:
- `_kl`, `_expect_p`, and `kl(snapshot, out)`: the exact chain-rule KL, mirroring `entropy()`.

battle_model/optim.py: class `SignalShare(decay=0.98)`:
- `state_dict` / `load_state_dict` (the `loaded` set).
- `read(optimizer)`:
  - A param with no grad counts as zeros.
  - S is bias-corrected: `mean / (1 - decay**count)`.
  - Readings: `signal_share_<group> = (g·S)/|g|²`; `signal_share_applied_<group>` with both divided by `sqrt(v̂)+eps`.
  - The mean is updated after the reading.
  - An S that doesn't fit after a load prints one line and starts cold.
  - On an exception it prints "signal_share: FAILURE ..." and sets `on = False`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

**Night term (run-away prior):**
- Night term = [P(lost tonight where the day ends) − P(where it stands)] × (army + hero + replacement), in gold.
- It sits in the logit priors only: tile_prior and travel_logits. The network inputs (move_features, travel_prior) are unchanged, a rule learned by measurement.
- Shelter rules: features.sheltered_danger and shelter_open. These match VCMI's CGTownInstance::onHeroVisit and isBattleOutsideTown:
  - A visitor with no garrison hero defends behind the walls with mergeGarrisonOnSiege.
  - A visitor beside a garrison hero fights outside.
- signed_offer for the MOVE and TRAVEL type offers.
- Towns' night (features.town_night), with our only town priced at ≥ Φ.
- Schema 18: travel_stop = (z×256 + y)×256 + x + 1, from the C++ CGPath (nodes[0] = target; the first node with turns == 0 from the target end is the stop).

**Measurement and stats:**
- Paired evaluation on pinned maps: make_map regenerates maps on each call. Inverse-variance pooling.
- The night reading's calibration: P^3.18 fits kill rates (neutral in paired test).
- measure_a, measure_a2: the deep (night reading ≥ 0.6) vs shallow penalized entries' P across checkpoints.

**Value and valves:**
- PPG-style ideas.
- Value valves:
  - r_v = 7-median fresh value loss / EMA(50).
  - s scales the value gradient into the shared layers through a buffer.
  - k scales target_kl.
  - Rate ×1.25 per update.
- Adam's normalization means loss-weight scaling is undone on single-term parameters.

**Trunk experiment:** a free value trunk vs a frozen one, held-out EV split by game; a shuffled-label control.

**Teacher (NK2) labels:**
- Advice (type, index, cell).
- Teacher CE through chosen_logp with the teacher span.
- advice_weights.
- night_dropped label drop.

**Infrastructure:** CUDA graph buffers read in place.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

**Schema 19:**
- Inputs at the end with 0 as none and +1 shifts. Old records decode the missing tail as zeros (TAIL_STARTS starts at w_arrive_left).
- Wire is int32 for int inputs and uint8 for bool, so fractions are fixed point: score ×100, days ×1000, loss ×10000, danger ×1000.
- NK_PER_HERO = 16, NK = 128 rows, one block of rows per hero slot.

**NK2 fork (vcmi):**
- lastCandidates: planPass's tasks, scored at lastPlanTier.
- buildEvaluationContext made public; PriorityEvaluator and DangerHitMapAnalyzer get DLL_EXPORT.
- Advisor (AdviceTaken exception; NK2 logs it as an "Unknown exception in makeTurn" ERROR — harmless, and nothing counts ERROR lines).
- candidateInputs mapping:
  - **First step:** the destination's own MOVE entry if legal today, else the stop today, never another path tile.
  - **TRAVEL:** the destination's area, else the farthest legal one.
  - **Kinds** from EvaluationContext; explore kind via explorePriority.
  - **Threats:** DangerHitMapAnalyzer.
- planFirstStep fallback at a miss, flagged RECORD_FALLBACK = 64.
- g_nk_action / g_nk_index / g_nk_cell hold NK2's own choice.
- ALPHA_MAP_FOLLOW_ADVICE is a chance with its own RNG.

**Settings:**
- play.nk2_settings: inputs, plus GATHER advice when on.
- Dropout drawn from the seed: random.Random(seed·7919+19).

**e5's model side (in e005793):**
- views.py: candidate_tables, value_views, etc.
- known.advice and known.tile_value.
- view_move / view_travel are FastLinear readouts (×10).
- Candidate prior: ALPHA_NK2_PRIOR / _FALLBACK.
- Label prior: ALPHA_NK2_LABEL.
- Mixture: config.nk2_follow / ALPHA_NK2_FOLLOW, P = rho·NK2's choice + (1−rho)·ours.
- Deploy 57 run line: nk2_inputs on, nk2_follow 0.9, teacher off (campaign.nk2_teacher False), value_jump_from 3107, valves off.

**Measurement method:**
- Paired eval on 48 pinned setups (paired_eval.py).
- Readout fits on caches with trainer-equivalent CE: chosen_logp, accept sets, TEACHER_SPAN, advice_weights.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

- **Follow mixture:** P = rho·NK2 + (1−rho)·ours.
  - At followed rows our gradient is scaled by ~P_own/9.
  - The cap is shift = moved − relu(moved).detach().
  - Extended to questions (g_nk_action 0, g_nk_index = option+1).
- **Advantage head:** AdvantageHead with adv_type, adv_move, adv_travel, adv_named, unnamed, fore.
  - Its loss is the squared error against the standardized advantage, on credited rows; inputs detached; in the value group.
  - Stage 2a: gain × clipped centered terms on our own logits, before the mixture.
- **Own games:** ALPHA_MAP_NK2_CHOICE=0, the per-game draw (seed·7907+59), choice_hidden(game), per-kind readings, adv_by_kind (standardization within each kind).
- **Split eval:** plan() gives OWN 0–31 and FOLLOW 16 (every other one by seat within player counts, 6/5/5); split_row; SHOWN_ONLY left empty; eval_score() returns score_own; arms pair separately; idle_movement_own/_follow.
- **Bundle 60 C++ switches** (all default 1):
  - ALPHA_MAP_REVISIT_SKIP: skip a Defend no-op re-visit, holding the hero via fork Nullkiller::adviceHeld; NK2's executingTask is recorded.
  - ALPHA_MAP_HOLD_DAY: heldToday.
  - ALPHA_MAP_REVISIT: the centre-cell entry on an unseen-exit entrance (probe) or a map shipyard (now the purchase itself, cb->buildBoat).
  - ALPHA_MAP_BOAT_ADVICE: the BuildBoat match plus the advised YES.
  - ALPHA_MAP_LOOP_GUARD: an action returning to a state seen today is dropped for the day (seenToday/loopsToday).
- **probes_no_way:** a probe that finds no way is not a failed action.
- **Fingerprint:** includes artifact instances and each town's garrison/visiting heroes.
- **EQUIP:** a free fitting slot first; no swap of like for like; the Python prior reads the same slot via artifact_slots.
- **Town guard:** features.town_guard_cost = size × gain(¼..2) × value_logit(relu(left_danger − town_danger) × stakes), gated by t_nk_threat (has, turns ≤ 1), on the visitor's MOVE/TRAVEL.
- **Forecast-out:** A fore_fight (FORE_FIGHT=4), B fore_move, C fore_answer (FORE_MOVE = 3 + parts), D fore_hero (zero_weight(2, d)); consequence_channels() and fight_forecast(); exp−1 instead of expm1 (ONNX opset 17).
- **Credit segments (C), planned:**
  - Segments 0–2, 2–5, 5–7 from per-offset targets and heads (VALUE_OFFSETS = (2, 5, 7)).
  - credit_evidence per segment; F = Var_t(m_t) / mean_t(v_t).
- **Deploy mechanics:** drain_restart.sh; gates dry-run on a copy of the LIVE checkpoint; gate on configured buffers, not learned values; the Mac worker is built from the main tree's build via mac_worker_build.sh.

**Key code, the C target in campaign_model/train.py (ppo_update schedule block):**
```
if config.credit == "schedule" and growth_real is not None:
    real7 = growth_real[:, -1]; grow = (real7 - log_phi).clamp(...) - (sigmoid(growth_now)*2*GROWTH_CAP - GROWTH_CAP)
    real_change = targets[:, -1]*2*SHARE_CHANGE_CAP - SHARE_CHANGE_CAP
    predicted_change = sigmoid(value_now)*2*SHARE_CHANGE_CAP - SHARE_CHANGE_CAP
    change = real_change - share_baseline(real_change, predicted_change, share_trust)
    ... w = share_weight(day, side, config); advantage = w*change + (1-w)*grow
```
- The prepass stores only `growth_now[index] = out["growth_logit"][:, -1]` and `value_now[index] = out["value_logit"][:, -1]` (train.py ~1265). Segments need all offsets.

- labels.credit_evidence(decisions, errors, players, evidence, memory) → {type: [n, sum, sumsq]}; credit_gaps; credit_trust.

My planned design:
- config.credit_segments (default False).
- The prepass stores value_logit and growth_logit for all offsets when on.
- Segments:
  - share c_k = real_k − share_baseline(real_k, pred_k, share_trust) for k in 2, 5, 7; growth g_k likewise;
  - seg = [c2, c5−c2, c7−c5] blended w·s + (1−w)·g;
  - advantage = seg0 + Σ where(finite, k_j·seg_j, 0).
- ppo_update gets a new arg segment_k=(k5, k7) and returns metrics["segment_surprise"] [n, 3].
- The learner builds a players array always when on, updates three evidence dicts, computes F and k_target over types with ≥ min_players, applies the EMA, and saves/restores segment_k and segment_evidence.

```cpp
if(!turn) return;
for(int slot = 0; slot < OWN_HEROES; ++slot) {
  const int hero = decision.windowHero[slot]; if(hero < 0) continue;
  const auto * h = cb->getHero(decision.heroes[hero]); if(!h || h->isGarrisoned()) continue;
  const auto info = paths.getPathsInfo(h);
  std::map<const CGPathNode *, const CGPathNode *> stopOf;
  for(row..) for(column..) { pos = decision.windowTile(slot,row,column); if(!isInTheMap||!isVisible) continue;
    node = info->getPathInfo(pos); if(!node||!node->reachable()||node->turns<1) continue;
    walk at=node while(at && at->turns>0 && !stopOf.count(at)) {walked.push_back(at); at=at->theNodeBefore;}
    stop = !at ? nullptr : at->turns==0 ? at : stopOf[at]; for(n:walked) stopOf[n]=stop;
    entry = stop ? decision.moveEntry(hero, stop->coord) : -1; if(entry>=0) in.at(In::w_stop,slot,row,column)=entry+1; } }
```

```python
if self.nk_read == "gate":
    toward_move = self.move_toward(pairs) * self.candidate_gate(pairs, x, known).unsqueeze(-1).to(pairs.dtype)
else:
    toward_move = self.move_toward(toward(*self.advice_for(known, "toward", "move").move_dest))
state.move_pairs = self.move_pair(torch.cat([pairs + toward_move, hidden["move"], move_known, mixed], dim=-1))
```
- `candidate_gate(pairs, x, known)`: sigmoid(head(pairs)) × (move_mask by known.windows & known.real), under no_grad.
